70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    74\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    75\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    76\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    77\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    78\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    85\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    86\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    87\t  def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    97\t        self.it = 0\n    98\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    99\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   100\t max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   105\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   106\t\n   107\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   112\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   113\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   114\t exploration, jade later for convergence\n   127\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   128\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   129\t        else:\n   130\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   131\t\n   132\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   133\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   134\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   135\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   136\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   137\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   138\t\n   139\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   140\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   141\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   142\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   143\t Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\",\n        \"new_str\": \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # JADE archives and parameter memories\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim))\\n        max_archive_size = pop_size\\n        mean_F = float(self.F)\\n        mean_CR = float(self.CR)\\n        c = 0.1  # adaptation rate\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller may provide a generation-level F/CR hint\\n            if 'F' in params:\\n                mean_F = float(params['F'])\\n            if 'CR' in params:\\n                mean_CR = float(params['CR'])\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_pool_size = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\\n            \\n            # Combine population and archive for mutation\\n            combined = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            combined_size = len(combined)\\n            \\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                \\n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_pool_size individuals\\n                pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                # Avoid selecting the same individual for pbest if possible\\n                if pbest_i == i and pbest_pool_size > 1:\\n                    tries = 0\\n                    while pbest_i == i and tries < 10:\\n                        pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                        tries += 1\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_i]\\n                \\n                # Choose r1 from the current population, excluding i\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1 = candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))]\\n                \\n                # Choose r2 from the combined population+archive\\n                # Avoid using the same vector as i if possible\\n                r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                tries = 0\\n                while tries < 10 and combined_size > 1:\\n                    same_as_i = False\\n                    if r2 < pop_size and r2 == i:\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    elif r2 >= pop_size and np.array_equal(combined[r2], pop[i]):\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    if not same_as_i:\\n                        break\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                    tries += 1\\n                if tries >= 10:\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                \\n                # Generate adaptive mutation/crossover factors using JADE\\n                F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(2.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(mean_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - combined[r2])\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\\n                u = pop[i].copy()\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect, then re-initialize if still outside\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                    elif u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                \\n                # Evaluate trial vector\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection with archive update\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\\n                    if len(archive) >= max_archive_size:\\n                        remove_idx = rng.randint(0, len(archive))\\n                        archive = np.delete(archive, remove_idx, axis=0)\\n                    archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = u\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n                    \\n                    success_F.append(F_i)\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_i)\\n            \\n            # Adapt F and CR memories based on successful generations\\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\\n                # Lehmer mean for F\\n                sum_F_sq = float(np.sum(np.square(success_F)))\\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(success_F))\\n                if sum_F > 0:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F + c * (sum_F_sq / sum_F)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F\\n                mean_CR = (1.0 - c) * mean_CR + c * float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\", 'new_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # JADE archives and parameter memories\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim))\\n        max_archive_size = pop_size\\n        mean_F = float(self.F)\\n        mean_CR = float(self.CR)\\n        c = 0.1  # adaptation rate\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller may provide a generation-level F/CR hint\\n            if 'F' in params:\\n                mean_F = float(params['F'])\\n            if 'CR' in params:\\n                mean_CR = float(params['CR'])\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_pool_size = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\\n            \\n            # Combine population and archive for mutation\\n            combined = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            combined_size = len(combined)\\n            \\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                \\n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_pool_size individuals\\n                pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                # Avoid selecting the same individual for pbest if possible\\n                if pbest_i == i and pbest_pool_size > 1:\\n                    tries = 0\\n                    while pbest_i == i and tries < 10:\\n                        pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                        tries += 1\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_i]\\n                \\n                # Choose r1 from the current population, excluding i\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1 = candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))]\\n                \\n                # Choose r2 from the combined population+archive\\n                # Avoid using the same vector as i if possible\\n                r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                tries = 0\\n                while tries < 10 and combined_size > 1:\\n                    same_as_i = False\\n                    if r2 < pop_size and r2 == i:\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    elif r2 >= pop_size and np.array_equal(combined[r2], pop[i]):\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    if not same_as_i:\\n                        break\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                    tries += 1\\n                if tries >= 10:\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                \\n                # Generate adaptive mutation/crossover factors using JADE\\n                F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(2.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(mean_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - combined[r2])\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\\n                u = pop[i].copy()\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect, then re-initialize if still outside\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                    elif u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                \\n                # Evaluate trial vector\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection with archive update\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\\n                    if len(archive) >= max_archive_size:\\n                        remove_idx = rng.randint(0, len(archive))\\n                        archive = np.delete(archive, remove_idx, axis=0)\\n                    archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = u\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n                    \\n                    success_F.append(F_i)\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_i)\\n            \\n            # Adapt F and CR memories based on successful generations\\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\\n                # Lehmer mean for F\\n                sum_F_sq = float(np.sum(np.square(success_F)))\\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(success_F))\\n                if sum_F > 0:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F + c * (sum_F_sq / sum_F)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F\\n                mean_CR = (1.0 - c) * mean_CR + c * float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"The file /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py has been edited. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'str_replace', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py', 'old_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Generate trial vectors\\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                if len(candidates) < 3:\\n                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\\n                else:\\n                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\\n                \\n                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\\n                \\n                # Mutation and crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\\n                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\", 'new_str': \"    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        if self.controller is not None:\\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\\n\\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\\n        dim = self.dim\\n        \\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\\n\\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\\n        \\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n        \\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n        \\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # JADE archives and parameter memories\\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim))\\n        max_archive_size = pop_size\\n        mean_F = float(self.F)\\n        mean_CR = float(self.CR)\\n        c = 0.1  # adaptation rate\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n        \\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n            \\n            # Controller may provide a generation-level F/CR hint\\n            if 'F' in params:\\n                mean_F = float(params['F'])\\n            if 'CR' in params:\\n                mean_CR = float(params['CR'])\\n            \\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n            \\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\\n            pbest_pool_size = max(1, int(pop_size * 0.15))\\n            \\n            # Combine population and archive for mutation\\n            combined = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\\n            combined_size = len(combined)\\n            \\n            success_F = []\\n            success_CR = []\\n            \\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                \\n                # Choose pbest from top pbest_pool_size individuals\\n                pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                # Avoid selecting the same individual for pbest if possible\\n                if pbest_i == i and pbest_pool_size > 1:\\n                    tries = 0\\n                    while pbest_i == i and tries < 10:\\n                        pbest_i = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, pbest_pool_size)]\\n                        tries += 1\\n                pbest = pop[pbest_i]\\n                \\n                # Choose r1 from the current population, excluding i\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1 = candidates[rng.randint(0, len(candidates))]\\n                \\n                # Choose r2 from the combined population+archive\\n                # Avoid using the same vector as i if possible\\n                r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                tries = 0\\n                while tries < 10 and combined_size > 1:\\n                    same_as_i = False\\n                    if r2 < pop_size and r2 == i:\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    elif r2 >= pop_size and np.array_equal(combined[r2], pop[i]):\\n                        same_as_i = True\\n                    if not same_as_i:\\n                        break\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                    tries += 1\\n                if tries >= 10:\\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, combined_size)\\n                \\n                # Generate adaptive mutation/crossover factors using JADE\\n                F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                while F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = mean_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                F_i = min(2.0, F_i)\\n                \\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(mean_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                \\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                v = pop[i] + F_i * (pbest - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - combined[r2])\\n                \\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\\n                u = pop[i].copy()\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n                \\n                # Boundary handling: reflect, then re-initialize if still outside\\n                for j in range(dim):\\n                    if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * lb[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                    elif u[j] > ub[j]:\\n                        u[j] = 2.0 * ub[j] - u[j]\\n                        if u[j] < lb[j]:\\n                            u[j] = rng.uniform(lb[j], ub[j])\\n                \\n                # Evaluate trial vector\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection with archive update\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\\n                    if len(archive) >= max_archive_size:\\n                        remove_idx = rng.randint(0, len(archive))\\n                        archive = np.delete(archive, remove_idx, axis=0)\\n                    archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\\n                    \\n                    pop[i] = u\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n                    \\n                    success_F.append(F_i)\\n                    success_CR.append(CR_i)\\n            \\n            # Adapt F and CR memories based on successful generations\\n            if len(success_F) > 0:\\n                # Lehmer mean for F\\n                sum_F_sq = float(np.sum(np.square(success_F)))\\n                sum_F = float(np.sum(success_F))\\n                if sum_F > 0:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F + c * (sum_F_sq / sum_F)\\n                else:\\n                    mean_F = (1.0 - c) * mean_F\\n                mean_CR = (1.0 - c) * mean_CR + c * float(np.mean(success_CR))\\n            \\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n\\n        self.stats = {\\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\\n            'evals': int(evals),\\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\\n        }\\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"The file /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py has been edited.
